# CHANGELOG

Append-only. One line per meaningful change. Add the short commit hash once committed.
Versioning is by git tag; this file is the human-readable "what shipped" ledger.

## Unreleased

- 2026-08-23 | Fix | An issued day's class note could not be edited at all (D-#531). `updateHomeworkItem` rejected on the PRESENCE of a frozen field rather than a change to it, and `ClassNoteHomeworkService` always sent `qCount` plus a fabricated `revItem: false` — so every save of an already-issued note failed with "TIME_DECL, Q_COUNT, revision flag is frozen", naming three fields the teacher had not touched and blocking the description/topics/attachments the rule explicitly allows. The guard now compares against stored values (arrays by content, absent/null/"" as one no-pool value) and names only what genuinely differs; the bridge forwards `qCount`/`revItem`/`timeDecl`/`poolRef` only when supplied. That second half also fixes a silent data loss: on a still-declared item the fabricated flag cleared REV_ITEM on every note save, with no error. Gate GREEN: vocab verifier PASS, server + app tsc, jest 3552/3552 (199 suites) with 11 new. [fix/classnote-issued-homework]
- 2026-08-23 | Contract | A `descriptive` question may now carry a `model_answer` instead of a `rubric` (D-#532). Amends the LOCKED QuestionPayload v1 additively (`envelope_version` unchanged): new optional `model_answer { text, key_points? }`, the descriptive branch becomes `anyOf: [rubric, model_answer]`, and `model_answer` is forbidden on the other five question types. validate_import.py L4 gains an actionable message for the neither-carrier case. The app `AnswerCarrier` and the answer-key PDF now RENDER a descriptive answer — both previously printed a pointer to a rubric that has no renderer. Unblocks the 18 বড় প্রশ্ন items of C5_BAN_17 that had nowhere to put their model answer.
- 2026-08-23 | Feature | Question review shows the ANSWER by default and takes a bulk verdict (D-#530). The queue card renders MCQ options with the correct one ticked, fill-in-the-blank accepted values and the short-answer key/model note via the existing `AnswerCarrier` — `payloadJson` was already being fetched and thrown away, so of one reviewer’s 241 chapter-17 questions, the 48 MCQ and 32 fill-blank ones were being judged with no sight of the answer. New `submitQuestionReviewBulk` (`content:review`) plus a select / select-all row and one shared optional reason; it refuses APPROVE_WITH_CONDITION (a condition belongs to ONE question), de-dupes ids, and collects per-item failures so a single closed round cannot discard the rest. Gate GREEN: vocab verifier PASS, server + app tsc, jest 3584/3584 (199 suites) with 7 new, expo web export exit 0. [feat/question-review-bulk]
- 2026-08-23 | Fix | A TEACHER whose `observation:review` was revoked in the access editor (AC-1) could not use the app AT ALL: the drawer fires `myVideoReviews` for every teacher, Pothos refused the field, GraphQL returned it as `null` INSIDE `data`, and `data?.myVideoReviews.length` threw `Cannot read properties of null` in AppTabs — the ErrorBoundary then replaced the whole app with কিছু একটা সমস্যা হয়েছে. Confirmed from prod logs (`ForbiddenError: Not authorized to resolve Query.myVideoReviews`). The probe is now paused unless the caller actually HOLDS the permission, and all three drawer probes read their list with `?.`, so no refused or absent field can take down the navigator again. Gate GREEN: vocab verifier + app tsc + web export (exit 0). [fix/drawer-crash-forbidden-probe]
- 2026-07-23 | Feature | Homework recording redesigned as ROSTER PASSES (RP-1/RP-2, D-#355, supersedes D-#267): one card per subject×date runs জমা (chip pass) → যাচাই (individual check) → ফেরত (chip pass), attendance-style (cross only the exceptions, commit once). New homeworkSubmitPass/homeworkReturnPass mutations (orchestrators over transitionRecord — no engine/vocab change); submit fast-forwards uncrossed → SUBMITTED with one timestamp and chases crossed FIRST-CROSS-ONLY (already-CHASE re-cross = no-op, no increment/notify); recordHomeworkOutcome NOT_SUBMITTED gains the same rule. New HomeworkWorkspaceScreen + reusable RosterChipPass + CardGrid (responsive laptop columns); homeworkOpenRecords/OpenRecordDTO gain hwItemId. Records + Checking screens retired (routes redirect one release). Gate GREEN: server tsc + 2121 jest (10 new homeworkRosterPass + updated homeworkOutcome) + vocab verifier; app tsc + web export. [feat/rp-homework-roster-pass]
- 2026-07-23 | Feature | Assignment tracker gets roster-pass parity (RP-3/RP-4, D-#356, mirrors D-#355) with chase DECOUPLED from the due date. New assignmentOpenRecords (section-wide, studentName inline) + assignmentSubmitPass/assignmentReturnPass (same first-cross-only rule, chase on cross regardless of due date — fixes G7) + recordAssignmentOutcome (marks + feedback, NEVER auto-spawns a resubmission, D-#87). New AssignmentWorkspaceScreen (route sectionId/classId from an AssignmentHome cell, grouped by asItem = subject×week; reuses RosterChipPass + CardGrid); Collect + Checking retired (redirect one release). sweepAssignmentChases stays dormant. Owner's 20/15/5 flow is a literal test. Gate GREEN: server tsc + 2121 jest (10 new assignmentRosterPass) + verifier; app tsc + web export. [feat/rp-homework-roster-pass]
- 2026-07-23 - fix(app): Assignment declare pending report labels assignment groups by delivery-month week, not the internal continuous `weekNumber` - the prod report showed `Week 3 - 2026-07-19` while rows delivered on `2026-07-23`; per D-#331, the user-facing label anchors on the delivery Thursday, so this now renders July Week 4 from `deliveryDateKey`. App-only; `weekNumber` remains the server key. Gate GREEN: app typecheck. [fix/as-pending-week-label]

- 2026-07-23 — feat(trackers): audit lifecycle REVERTs — the one action that erased its own evidence (D-#354) — `popActionGroup` deletes the popped `stateDates` stamps, so a reverted record was byte-identical to one never worked on, and no homework/assignment lifecycle event existed in the audit log at all. Surfaced by the "Fida marked it all, report says pending" investigation, which the data could not close either way. New `HW_RECORD_REVERTED` / `AS_RECORD_REVERTED` audit kinds written by both revert services, carrying item + student, `revertedFrom` → `restoredTo`, admin flag, and the full popped stamp list (state/at/by) so the undone work stays legible. Scoped to revert only — forward moves are already self-documenting in `stateDates`, and per-transition rows would balloon (21 per bulk mark). No app change needed: the Audit Log screen derives its kind filter from returned rows and already renders `metaJson`. Gate GREEN: server tsc, server jest 2099/2099 (127 suites, +2). [feat/revert-audit]

- 2026-07-23 — feat(trackers): class tests attribute to the accountable SUBJECT TEACHER, not the creator (D-#352) — a Principal/Office registration on a teacher's behalf showed the admin's name on the Class-test report, because `ClassTest` had only `requestedBy` (both "who entered it" and "whose exam it is"). New optional `ClassTest.teacherId` set **explicit pick → routine → actor**, with `requestedBy` kept as the audit trail; Request form gains a "বিষয় শিক্ষক" select (default "রুটিন অনুযায়ী"). Report attributes/filters on `teacherId ?? requestedBy`, `listMyClassTests` = subject-teacher ∪ requester, publish notification routes to the subject teacher; result entry still open to subject teacher + admin. The D-#351 routine walk moved to `trackers/subjectTeacher.ts` so homework + class tests share one rule. Existing rows: `server/scripts/backfill-classtest-teacher.ts` (dry-run default, `--apply` to persist). Gate GREEN: shared build, server+app tsc, server jest 2086/2086 (+3), expo web export exit 0. [feat/ct-subject-teacher]

- 2026-07-23 — fix(app): assignment detail screens label the week like the Assignments home (owner finding on prod) — the home showed "July · Week 4" but ⚖️ Weekly load opened "Week 3". Three correct-but-different numberings exist: `weekOfMonth` (the home label), `cycleWeek` (rotation), and the continuous term-anchored `weekNumber` (the canonical server key). `AssignmentReconcileScreen` + `DeliverAssignmentScreen` printed the raw continuous index — a number shown nowhere else — though both loaded the right week. The home now passes `month` + `weekOfMonth` as optional route params and both detail screens render "July · Week 4", falling back to the raw index when absent. App-only; no server change, no new STR key; `weekNumber` remains the query/mutation key. Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [fix/as-week-label]

- 2026-07-22 — fix(app): Class-test report "Published" status badge (owner ask) — published exams read as merely "Complete" on both the Principal/Office Class-test report (`ClassTestReportScreen`) and the teacher Reports-Status list (`ClassTestReportsScreen`). Now when a row's `publishedAt` is set the status badge shows "প্রকাশিত"/"Published" (tone ok) — the terminal state past complete — and the admin report's detail line appends the published date next to submitted. App-only: the row already carries `publishedAt` (server unchanged); reuses the existing `ctPublishedBadge` label. Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [fix/ct-report-published-badge]

- 2026-07-22 — feat(app): English Drive editor two-channel bridge (owner ask) — the WYSIWYG editor now offers BOTH destinations: the existing "Office print queue →" (normal print, jumps to NewPrintRequest, source UPLOAD) and a new "Class test →" button that jumps to the Class Test "Request" flow (`ClassTestTab → RequestClassTest`), where the teacher uploads the saved PDF as the exam paper for a tracked class-test cycle. Both are Save-as-PDF bridges (edited worksheets print locally); shared hint names both. New STR key `edSendToClassTest`. No server change (Class Test already accepts UPLOADED_PAPER); reuses the queue-bridge pattern. Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [feat/english-drive-classtest-bridge]

- 2026-07-22 — feat(app): English Drive editor → office print-queue bridge (D-#349) — after WYSIWYG editing, an "Office print queue →" button + 2-step hint (Print/preview → Save as PDF → this button → upload the PDF) jumps to the Print tab's NewPrintRequest (source defaults to UPLOAD) with the worksheet title prefilled, so the edited version can go through the office queue. Reuses the existing print-request flow; no server change. Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [feat/english-drive-editor-queue-bridge]

- 2026-07-22 — fix(app): English Drive WYSIWYG editor/print parity (D-#349, owner feedback) — the editor and Print/preview looked different (editor used inline 14px styles; print a separate 12pt stylesheet). Now one shared `worksheetCss(sel, lineHeight)` styles both (`.scd-ws` for the editor via injected `<style>`, `body` for the print doc), and the editor page is a real A4 page (794px @96dpi with a 16mm inset, box-sizing border-box) so its content width matches the print @page — same font/table/heading/list rules → WYSIWYG. Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [fix/english-drive-editor-wysiwyg-parity]

- 2026-07-22 — fix(app): English Drive WYSIWYG editor list auto-numbering + Tab/space (D-#349, owner test findings) — markdown `1. 2. 3.` rendered as a real `<ol>`, which contentEditable auto-numbers (Enter made a new list item, numbers renumbered, couldn't type/delete your own). Now flatten `<ol>/<ul>` to plain `<div>` lines with the marker as editable text on load (`flattenListsHtml`); Tab inserts a real tab and the editor + print use `white-space: pre-wrap` so tabs/spaces are preserved for manual alignment. Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [fix/english-drive-editor-lists]

- 2026-07-22 — fix(app): English Drive WYSIWYG editor toolbar + print (D-#349, owner test findings) — the text-size/line-spacing `<select>` menus did nothing because the toolbar's container `onMouseDown preventDefault` blocked the dropdowns; moved preventDefault to the B/I/U buttons only and made the saved selection robust via a `selectionchange` listener. Print switched from `window.open` (popup-blocked) to a hidden-iframe print so the browser's print dialog (preview) always opens. Editor print button relabelled "Print / preview". Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [fix/english-drive-wysiwyg-toolbar]

- 2026-07-22 — feat(app): English Drive WYSIWYG worksheet editor (D-#349) — web-only `RichWorksheetEditor` (raw-DOM contentEditable seeded from the stored markdown via a new `markdown-it` app dep): Enter makes real lines, select text → font size, select a block/section → line spacing, bold/italic/underline, edit tables in place; Print opens the browser's own print dialog (A4 @page CSS, WYSIWYG). Edited worksheets print locally (not the office queue). Web opens this from "✎ সম্পাদনা করে প্রিন্ট"; native keeps the D-#348 markdown flow. No server change. Gate GREEN: app tsc, expo web export exit 0. [feat/english-drive-wysiwyg]

- 2026-07-22 — feat(app,server): English Drive edit-before-print polish (D-#348 follow-up) — layout controls (text size / line spacing / margin) in one flex-wrap row on wide screens; taller content box (new `Field` inputStyle prop); Google-Docs line-spacing presets (Single / 1.15 / 1.5 / Double) with an ADDITIVE renderer gap (`base + fontSize·(ls−1)`, so ls=1 stays byte-identical and Double actually looks double) + per-block `{ls:1.5}` / `{ls:reset}` directive (honoured + stripped by the renderer and the preview); the app Markdown preview now decodes HTML entities (`&emsp;` etc.) to match the PDF. Gate GREEN: server+app tsc, pdf jest (+1), full suite + expo export green. [feat/english-drive-edit-polish]

- 2026-07-22 — feat(app,server): English Drive edit-before-print + layout knobs (D-#348) — the pdfkit renderer is parametrized (`resolveLayout` → clamped fontScale/lineSpacing/margin; defaults reproduce the current output byte-for-byte, so artifact/set PDFs are unchanged); the doc viewer gains an "edit before print" mode (editable markdown box + font-size/line-spacing/margin presets) with a `POST /pdf/english-drive/render` preview and edited send-to-print (`sendEnglishDriveDocToPrint` gains optional contentMd+layout). Edits are one-off (not persisted). GET route accepts `?fontScale=&lineSpacing=&margin=`. No new permission/vocab. Gate GREEN: server+app tsc, server jest (+6), expo web export exit 0. - 2026-07-21 — fix(app,server): English Drive doc identity gains a per-kind `seq` (D-#345, owner test finding) — `C1B03_HW1..HW4` are four different homework sheets, but the (class, block, kind) replace key made each upload replace the previous. Identity/replace key is now (class, block, kind, seq); seq parses from digits glued to the kind token (HW4 → ৪, default ১), shows as an editable form field, numbers library rows when a block has several of one kind, and joins the print stamp. Filename parser relaxed to the real no-separator corpus naming (`C1B03CW1`, `GrammarBlock3`). Pre-seq rows read/replace as seq 1 — no migration. Gate GREEN: server+app tsc, server jest 2069/2069 (126 suites, +2), expo web export exit 0. [fix/english-drive-seq]

- 2026-07-05 — fix(hr): a teacher on leave (applied or approved) is now excluded from every cover picker and hard-refused at approval — new `userIdsOnLeave(dateKey)` drops them from `teacherAvailability` entirely and backstops `decideCoverSlot`. Live-testing find: previously an on-leave teacher still showed as "Free" and could be assigned to cover a different absent teacher's class. Gate GREEN: server tsc, jest 1589/1589. [fix/proxy-grant-future-expiry]
- 2026-07-05 — fix(routine): `slotsForTeacherOnDate` no longer hard-filters `groupType: "section"` — the leftover filter meant Quran/Arabic periods never reached cover fan-out even after the subjectgroup feature landed. Added a direct regression test (the unit suites mock this fn, which hid the bug). Gate GREEN: server tsc, jest 1584/1584. [fix/proxy-grant-future-expiry]
- 2026-07-05 — feat(hr): Quran/Arabic (subjectgroup) meetings now flow through leave cover. `StaffCoverSlot` gains a `groupType`: a section slot mints the usual proxy grant on approval, a subjectgroup slot is recorded + notified only (no content/tracker scope to grant, mirroring the R-4 precedent). Fan-out, decide, needs-cover, and the app displays (group name e.g. কায়দা) all handle both planes. Gate GREEN: server tsc, jest 1582/1582; app tsc, expo export. [fix/proxy-grant-future-expiry]
- 2026-07-05 — fix(hr): a still-pending cover proposal now reserves that teacher for the (date, period) too — `proposeCover`/`decideCoverSlot` reject a second teacher proposing/assigning the same colleague at the same meeting until the first is rejected. Gate GREEN: server tsc, jest 1580/1580. [fix/proxy-grant-future-expiry]
- 2026-07-05 — fix(hr): prevent double-booking a teacher across two cover slots at the same (date, period) — `teacherAvailability` now counts approved HR covers as busy, and `decideCoverSlot` hard-rejects a same-meeting conflict. Gate GREEN: server tsc, jest 1575/1575. [fix/proxy-grant-future-expiry]
- 2026-07-05 — feat(routine): a covering teacher now sees the HR proxy period on their Today/My-Routine screen (`myDay` pulls approved cover slots, badged "কভার করছেন" with the absent teacher's name). A proxy grant previously scoped only access, never visibility. Gate GREEN: server tsc, jest 1572/1572; app tsc, expo export. [fix/proxy-grant-future-expiry]
- 2026-07-05 — fix(foundation): future-dated proxy grants no longer die before they start — `composeTeacherScope` treated "not yet started" as "expired" and permanently killed a next-day cover grant on first scope-resolve. New `isProxyExpired()` distinguishes not-started from elapsed. Found via the per-meeting cover redesign (later-day grants have real future start dates). Gate GREEN: server tsc, jest 1569/1569. [fix/proxy-grant-future-expiry]
- 2026-07-04 — feat(hr): PXG-2 — `AvailableTeacherSelect` (free-first availability picker), `LeaveCoverScreen` gains an admin "অন্য কাউকে দিন" override control on proposed slots and a direct-assign picker on needs_cover slots, plus the new `NeedsCoverInboxScreen` (cross-leave worklist, route `NeedsCoverInbox`, entry button on `LeaveAdminScreen`). 5 new STR key pairs, verified correctly encoded in the exported bundle. - 2026-07-04 — feat(hr): PXG-1 — cover override/direct-assign + needs-cover inbox + availability-gate widening (D-#268). Live pre-flight against the PRD found its premises partly stale (COVER_ASSIGNED already existed; the target flow is the HR leave-cover module, not the routine R-4 flow) — resolved and documented, not silently patched. Per an explicit build-session ruling (D-#269), `fanOutCoverSlots` is redesigned from one slot per (section, subject) spanning the whole leave to one slot per actual (date, period) class meeting (derived from `RoutineSlot.teacherId`), each approval now minting its own one-day proxy grant. New `overrideCoverTeacherUserId` arg on `decideStaffCoverSlot` (additive), new `needsCoverSlots(from, to)` cross-leave read, `teacherAvailability` gate widened to any authenticated staff (guardians still excluded), new `emitHrCoverAssigned` reusing the existing `COVER_ASSIGNED` kind. No new permission; vocab verifier confirmed unchanged. Gate GREEN: server `tsc` clean, jest 1565/1565 (97 suites). [feat/pxg-1-cover-server]
- 2026-07-04 — feat(homework): HWG-2 — rebuilt `CheckingQueueScreen` as a one-tap outcome grid (D-#267): lists GIVEN/DUE/SUBMITTED/CHASE/CHECKED/RESUBMIT/ABSENT_REDELIVER (was SUBMITTED-only), grouped by date then by homework item. Four chips (ঠিক/আংশিক/ভুল/দেয়নি) call the new `recordHomeworkOutcome` mutation; ঠিক/দেয়নি fire immediately, আংশিক/ভুল expand an inline resubmit+top-up panel. Non-actionable rows render read-only with a রেকর্ডস hint back to Records. 6 new STR key pairs, verified correctly encoded in the exported bundle. Built on top of the app-wide encoding fix (fix/ux-testing-bugs, BUG-009/011/012). Gate GREEN: app `tsc --noEmit` clean, `expo export --platform web` green (1906 modules). [feat/hwg-2-check-grid]
- 2026-07-04 — feat(homework): HWG-1 — new composite mutation `recordHomeworkOutcome` (D-#267): fast-forwards a record's lifecycle (GIVEN→DUE→SUBMITTED or →CHASE) then applies the existing check/chase logic verbatim, via a new `HomeworkOutcomeService` orchestrating `transitionRecord`/`checkRecord` with zero edge-logic duplication. Also exposes `result` on `homeworkOpenRecords` (additive field the HWG-2 grid needs for read-only rows). No new permission/vocab/contract change. - 2026-07-03 — fix(i18n): repair the app-wide UTF-8→CP1252 double-encoding corruption (BUG-009 + BUG-011 + BUG-012). - feat(comments): teachers author/edit comments, Principal/Office review + deliver from a dashboard (server + app, **D-#263**) — from live testing. **Authoring** is no longer section-scoped: `recordStudentComment` drops `assertCanWrite` (keeps `tracker:write`; still server-resolves/stores the section) so any teacher can comment on any child; a teacher edits **their own** + sees them in a new **"My comments"** list (`myStudentComments`). **Delivery to guardians is removed from the teacher end** and re-gated to **Principal/Office** (`roster:manage`): `deliverStudentComment` is `roster:manage`-only, `editStudentComment` now allows the author OR a P/O reviewer (sealed once delivered), and a new **`commentReviewInbox`** (`roster:manage`) drives a **Review & deliver dashboard** of every undelivered comment (child + author names) where P/O review, edit, then deliver. Section-timeline reads widened `tracker:read`→`authenticated` so Office (which lacks tracker perms) can review — `assertReadSection` stays the gate (guardians/out-of-scope teachers still blocked). **No new permission / enum / vocab / contract** (reuses `tracker:write`/`roster:manage`); identity-plane only, firewall untouched. **Gate GREEN (executed against dev base):** vocab verifier PASS, shared build + shared/server tsc clean, app tsc clean, **server jest green** (new `myComments` / `reviewInbox` / reviewer-edit tests), expo web export green. - fix(class-test): CT-4 dashboard/reports RBAC — the four READ aggregates now gate `authScopes: { authenticated: true }` and let the resolver gate helpers enforce access (the `assertChaseAdmin` pattern), instead of `{ hasPermission: "tracker:read" }` (server-only, vocab-free, **NO permission change**, D-#196 [renumbered from #186 at merge — Finance REQ took #186]). **The pre-existing bug** (flagged at the CT-5 app review): OFFICE legitimately reads the dashboard + reports (§6/§9, the D-#166 intent) but holds NO `tracker:read` — it holds `message:dispatch` (which is why the overdue-chase already worked) — so Pothos scope-auth rejected Office at the `authScopes` layer BEFORE the resolver's Principal/Office gate (`assertDashboardAdmin`/`assertReportRead`) could run, making the OFFICE branch dead code. Fix: `classTestPrincipalDashboard` keeps `assertDashboardAdmin` (P/O) now reachable by Office; `classTestReportsStatus` / `classTestClassSubjectAnalysis` / `classTestStudentProfile` keep `assertReportRead` (P/O unscoped; teacher section-scoped via `assertCanRead`; GUARDIAN + any role without `tracker:read` denied). Non-widening: the chase (`message:dispatch` + P/O) is unchanged; the school-wide dashboard stays P/O-only; teacher reads stay section-scoped; GUARDIAN/unauthenticated stay denied. New schema-execution test `classTestSummaryRbac.test.ts` (11 tests) runs real GraphQL queries against the built schema with each role's context — exercising the scope-auth layer + the real permission map — proving Office reads all four, a teacher reads only a section it can read (and is denied the dashboard), and GUARDIAN/unauthenticated are denied. **Gate GREEN (executed):** vocab verifier PASS (untouched), shared build + shared/server tsc clean, **jest 1090/1090** (65 suites; +1 new suite `classTestSummaryRbac.test.ts` [11]; the existing `classTestSummary.test.ts` stays green). - Messaging M-6 — Principal oversight + guardian notice composer + dispatch seam (server, D-#77/#79/#111).
  **Flipped `chat:oversee` pipeline→build** in `PERMISSION_BUILD_STATUS` (the LAST pipeline perm — the
  verifier's pipeline-set is now EMPTY; §C.7 build-status assertion updated). **Oversight** (`chat:oversee`
  = PRINCIPAL only): `ChatOversightService` — `oversightConversations` (EVERY conversation incl. DIRECT +
  archived, no membership filter), `oversightMessages` (UN-MASKED — Principal sees deleted originals, since
  M-3 delete only stamps `deletedAt`), `openConversationOversight` (the audited open, `CHAT_OVERSIGHT_OPENED`,
  one row per open); read-only — post/edit/delete stay membership-gated. **Guardian notices** (`GuardianNotice`
  model + `composeGuardianNotice`): per-guardian ADR-003 wa.me fan-out (one link per active student WITH a
  family phone; phone-less → `unreachableCount`); authorization is the extracted, unit-tested
  `assertCanComposeNotice` (the D-#45 duty: SECTION → class teacher via `assertIsClassTeacher` OR chat:manage;
  SCHOOL → chat:manage; no new permission); `NOTICE_SENT` audited. **Dispatch seam**:
  `MessageDispatchService.dispatchSystemMessage(userId, text)` posts as a sentinel SYSTEM sender into a
  per-user system→user DIRECT thread (ANNOUNCEMENT one-way feed; privileged, bypasses membership/posting
  gates; idempotent on directKey) — the interface the routine triggers will call (wiring stays in the routine
  module's court). Two new audit kinds in `platform/models/Audit.ts`. Firewall extended (corpus ↛
  `models/GuardianNotice`). App surfaces (oversight browser + notice composer) are a later app pass. Gate
  GREEN (executed): vocab verifier PASS (chat:oversee flip), shared+server tsc clean, **jest 776/776**
  (48 suites; 17 new across chatOversight/guardianNotice/messageDispatch; firewall green), app tsc clean +
  expo web export green. Not verified live. Next = M-7 (staff Expo push).
- Messaging M-5 — Chat tab + screens (Expo app; APP-ONLY, no server/vocab/contract change). New 💬 **Chat
  tab** gated `chat:read` (Principal/Teacher/Office; GUARDIAN never sees it), a `ChatStack` with four
  screens consuming the existing M-1..M-4 server APIs: **ChatHome** (conversation list from
  `myConversations` — DIRECT + auto SECTION/SUBJECT/SCHOOL + manual CUSTOM, ANNOUNCEMENT badge, last
  activity; "+new DM" + managers' "new group"), **ChatThread** (messages with `_id`-cursor "load older",
  per-message reply / forward / react-toggle / edit / delete own-only, deleted → Bangla removed-placeholder,
  reactions aggregated by emoji, seen ✓count on own messages, `markSeen` on focus; ANNOUNCEMENT groups
  hide the composer for non-managers but keep reactions; attachment picker + viewer via `POST /files/chat`
  and `GET /files/:id`, web-only with graceful degrade), **NewChat** (1:1 picker), **GroupManage**
  (`chat:manage`: create group + posting policy, add/remove manual members, flip OPEN⇄ANNOUNCEMENT, archive
  CUSTOM). **The staff directory for the new-DM / add-member pickers is DERIVED from conversation
  memberships** (the SCHOOL auto-group holds every active staff member) — there is **no `chat:read`-scoped
  staff-directory query** on the server, and per the app-only guardrail I did not add one; this membership
  derivation is sufficient because SCHOOL contains all staff (flagged for the coordinator if a dedicated
  directory read is wanted later). New `lib/chat.ts` helpers + `pickAndUploadChatFile` in `lib/files.ts` +
  chat labels (BN/EN) in `lib/labels.ts`; follows the existing tab-nav / urql / `docs/ui-guidelines.md`
  token patterns (D-#61). Gate GREEN (executed): app `tsc --noEmit` clean + `expo export --platform web`
  green; no-drift confirmed — vocab verifier PASS + **jest 738/738** untouched (server unchanged). Not
  verified live. Next = M-6 (oversight + guardian notices — flips `chat:oversee`, a vocab-toucher).
- HR step 3 — payroll (server, prd-hr §4, D-#26/#27; build rulings D-#109/#110). New `modules/hr/` models
  `PayrollRun` (monthly; prepared → approved_locked, immutable once locked), `Payslip` (itemised net =
  gross − deductions + additions), `AdvanceLoan` (qard-hasan — interest- & fee-free, no rate/fee field).
  Services: `payrollMath` (pure dayRate + computePayslip with the §4.5 net-pay guard), `PayrollService`
  (prepare/recompute → approve+lock+commit-advance-recovery → cancel → paymentExport), `AdvanceService`
  (issue/settle/read). `StaffProfile` gains optional `monthlySalary` + `paymentMethod` (no migration);
  `setStaffPay` (payroll:manage) sets them. **RBAC (D-#109):** `payroll:manage` (PRINCIPAL/OFFICE) prepares/
  reads; `payroll:approve` (PRINCIPAL only) locks runs + issues/settles advances — Office cannot approve, a
  distinct permission the verifier proves. **Lock/correction seam (D-#110):** a locked run is never
  retro-edited — post-lock corrections ride `arrears`/clawback lines on the NEXT run; the unpaid-leave
  deduction reads the STORED leave paid/unpaid split (not the read-time attendance overlay); advance recovery
  commits at lock (recompute-safe); day-rate = monthlySalary ÷ run working days; cash-paid staff excluded
  from the payment export. Vocab (app-native, NO wire sync): `PAYMENT_METHODS`/`PAYROLL_RUN_STATUSES`/
  `PAY_DEDUCTION_TYPES`/`PAY_ADDITION_TYPES`/`ADVANCE_STATUSES` + BN/EN + `payroll:manage`/`payroll:approve`;
  verifier §C.9 + OFFICE exact-list updated. New audit kinds STAFF_PAY_SET/PAYROLL_PREPARED/PAYROLL_APPROVED/
  PAYROLL_CANCELLED/ADVANCE_ISSUED/ADVANCE_SETTLED. HR firewall block extended with the payroll models.
  Gate GREEN: vocab verifier PASS, shared+server tsc clean, jest 731/731 (44 suites; 19 new in payroll).
  Server-only; not verified live. Parked (prd-hr §10): entitlement/bonus figures, statutory deductions,
  payment-export target format, lateness-rule parameters, day-rate ÷30 alternative.
- Messaging M-4 — chat attachments: image/PDF/video/voice ≤10 MB (server, D-#108). **Storage REUSES the
  GP-A Google Drive store — the PRD §9 Oracle-VM-disk proposal is NOT built** (Drive already holds the
  bytes on the school's My-Drive quota; the VM-disk reason — GridFS can't hold video — is moot). No twin
  `Attachment` model/transport: generalized `DriveStore` (a `subfolder` param → `SCD-Hub-Files/<year>/chat/`)
  + `StoredFile` (four `chat_*` kinds added to the existing `hw_*` enum + an optional `conversationId`) +
  the existing `GET /files/:id` server-streamed transport (Drive id never reaches a client). New
  `POST /files/chat` (multipart, `chat:write` + `assertChatMember`, MIME whitelist per ATTACHMENT_KINDS +
  10 MB cap, Bangla 422, Drive-first ⇒ 503 + nothing persisted). New `ChatFileService`: read gate
  `assertChatFileReadAccess` (member of a conversation with a LIVE message referencing the file → a deleted
  message's attachment becomes inaccessible, the M-4 acceptance; refs stay in the MESSAGE_DELETED audit);
  `GET /files/:id` dispatches the gate by the file's OWN kind (hw → HomeworkFile, chat → ChatFile) so neither
  plane can re-expose the other's files. `sendMessage` gains `attachmentIds` — `resolveSendAttachments`
  admits only CHAT files the SENDER uploaded FOR this conversation (no foreign/cross-conversation/hw file);
  an attachment-only message (no body) is now allowed; `ChatMessage` GraphQL type gains an `attachments`
  field (batched per page). One new audit kind `CHAT_ATTACHMENT_UPLOADED` in `platform/models/Audit.ts`
  (NOT vocab — HR owns shared/vocab.ts this cycle). Gate GREEN (executed): vocab verifier PASS (untouched),
  shared+server tsc clean, **jest 710/710** (43 suites; 26 new in `chatAttachments.test.ts`; firewall green;
  homeworkFiles StoredFile mock extended for the generalized kinds), app tsc clean + expo web export green.
  Not verified live (Drive credential + DEP-3). Next = M-5 app screens.
- HR step 2 — staff LEAVE source + the staff-attendance leave reconciliation (server, prd-hr §3/H2,
  D-#22/#23; build rulings D-#102/#103). New `modules/hr/`: `StaffLeaveEntitlement` (per staff/year/type
  allowance — admin DATA, numbers parked, no seed), `StaffLeaveApplication` (parent record + paid/unpaid
  split; exceed WARNS, never blocks, §3.3; maternity/hajj wholly unpaid, D-#23), `StaffCoverSlot` (fans out
  one slot per class the absent teacher teaches → on approval mints a D-#20 proxy grant via assignProxy;

  helpers), CoverService (fan-out/propose/approve/revoke), staffMatch (the phone-only User↔StaffProfile join,
  no FK/migration added). **Closed the AT-1 ✘-resolution seam**: a biometric ✘ now reads LEAVE when an
  APPROVED staff leave covers that staff/date — a READ-TIME overlay in `TeacherAttendanceService`
  (forDate + summary), correct even when leave is approved after the snapshot import. Vocab (app-native, NO
  wire sync): `LEAVE_TYPES`/`LEAVE_STATUSES`/`COVER_SLOT_STATUSES` + BN/EN + `LEAVE_TYPE_RULES` (the settled
  §3.2 table) + `leave:manage` perm (PRINCIPAL/OFFICE, build); teacher own-row self-apply needs NO permission;
  verifier §C.8 added + OFFICE exact-list updated. New audit kinds STAFF_LEAVE_ENTITLEMENT_SET/SUBMITTED/
  DECIDED + STAFF_COVER_PROPOSED/DECIDED. Firewall test extended both ways (corpus ⇄ hr). **Scope boundary
  (D-#102):** the §3a punch/schedule/grace/working-days attendance model is NOT rebuilt — the live
  symbol-snapshot importer already is HR-2b's internal record + manual transport; punch-level §3a presupposes
  the parked live-device sync (D-#24/H7.6). Gate GREEN (executed): vocab verifier PASS, shared+server tsc
  clean, jest 690/690 (27 new in staffLeave + 2 firewall; firewall green). Server-only (no app screens, the
  Messaging-M-1/M-2 precedent); not verified live.
- Messaging M-3 — rich messaging: reply/forward/reactions/edit/delete (server, D-#77/#101). Wires the
  inert M-1 ChatMessage fields. **forward** (`forwardMessage`): sender must be a member of BOTH source +
  target; sets `forwardOfId`, carries attachment refs forward, honours the target's ANNOUNCEMENT posting
  policy (M-2 gate reused); a deleted source is rejected. **reactions** (new `Reaction` model + `toggleReaction`):
  ONE per user per message — same emoji toggles OFF, a different emoji SWITCHES (no controlled reaction-set
  enum, so vocab stays untouched); allowed in ANNOUNCEMENT groups, rejected on a deleted message;
  batched per page next to receipts (no N+1). **edit** (`editMessage`): own messages only; prior body written
  to the append-only audit (`MESSAGE_EDITED`) FIRST, then `editedAt` stamped; empty/deleted rejected; no time
  limit (Principal's choice). **delete** (`deleteMessage`): own only, hide-not-erase — original body +
  attachment refs retained in the audit (`MESSAGE_DELETED`), the row then masked behind a Bangla
  removed-placeholder for every reader (`listMessages`/`getChatMessage` mask; hard delete never occurs);
  re-delete is idempotent. All four reuse `assertChatMember` + `ChatError` + `writeAudit` (no twins).
  Resolvers `forwardMessage`/`editMessage`/`deleteMessage`/`toggleReaction` (chat:write + membership);
  ChatMessage type gains `deletedAt` + `reactions`. Two new audit kinds in `platform/models/Audit.ts`
  (NOT vocab — a parallel HR session owns shared/vocab.ts this cycle). Firewall test extended (corpus ↛
  `models/Reaction`). Gate GREEN (executed): vocab verifier PASS (untouched), shared+server tsc clean,
  **jest 683/683** (42 suites; 20 new in `chatRich.test.ts`; firewall green), app tsc clean + expo web
  export green. Not verified live. Next = M-4 attachments.
- Messaging M-3 coordinator-review fix — bound the free-form reaction emoji length (D-#101 keeps it
  enum-free, but it was UNbounded: a client could store an arbitrary multi-KB string as a "reaction").
  `Reaction.emoji` now `maxlength: 64` + a service-side guard in `toggleReaction` (rejects > 64 chars
  with a clean Bangla error before any DB write); 64 comfortably fits any single-emoji ZWJ grapheme.
  Other review findings judged not worth changing at this scale: the toggle's read-then-write (2 round-trips)
  and a not-yet-needed `(conversationId,userId)` Reaction index — left as-is; the "edit leaks deleted body"
  and ObjectId/string-cast flags were REFUTED (edit rejects deleted messages; Mongoose casts filters).
  Gate GREEN (executed): vocab verifier PASS, shared+server tsc clean, **jest 684/684** (+1 bound test),
  app tsc + expo web export green. [worktree-messaging-m3]

- App i18n — bilingual UI (Bangla + English); Bangla stays the default. Added `*_LABELS_EN` maps to
  `/shared/vocab.ts` mirroring every `*_LABELS_BN`; `app/src/lib/labels.ts` now resolves `STR` (a Proxy)
  and all label fns + `bnNum` by a module-level active language; new `LanguageProvider` persists the choice
  and keys only the nav subtree (auth/basket/section survive a switch); BN/EN toggle in the stack header +
  Login screen; routed 5 hardcoded Bangla strings through `STR`. Vocab verifier extended with EN-totality
  checks. Gates: verifier PASS, shared/server/app tsc clean, jest 223/223, web bundle green (483 modules).
- Homework Tracker — drafted the consult-via-human deviation note to Project 06 re the Quran exclusion
  (`docs/project06-deviation-quran.md`): D-#36 excludes Quran from `HW_SUBJECTS`, which deviates from handoff
  §6.2/§6.3 (Quran-homework rows were meant to live here + count in the 240-min weekday budget). The note
  states the conflict, asks the substantive questions (ceiling math, double-log guard, roster), notes the
  reversibility, and requests confirm-or-amend back through the Principal. Doc only; for the Principal to
  forward.
- Homework Tracker — assign-class-teacher screen (D-#42 UI): new `AssignClassTeacherScreen` in the Admin tab
  (`roster:manage`, Principal/Office) — pick a section, enter a TEACHER user id, assign (or clear) the
  section's class teacher (the daily coordinator who runs reconciliation/confirm, handoff §9). Shows the
  current class teacher. Adds the `assignClassTeacher` app op + exposes `Section.classTeacherId` on
  `CLASSES_QUERY`. Frontend-only, no server/contract change. Gate: app `tsc --noEmit` clean + web bundle
  green (482 modules). This closes the "no UI for class-teacher assignment" gap; the actual live assignment
  is still an operational step on a running deployment.
- Homework Tracker — principal roll-ups screen (frontend for §7.3/§7.4/§8.4): new `HomeworkRollupsScreen` in
  the Homework tab (reachable from HomeworkHome) — resubmission watch-list (≥3 in a rolling 2 weeks),
  per-subject trim-pattern flags for a month (>30% of reconciled days; month picker → first/last-day range),
  and the de-identified question-usage feed. Adds the `homeworkWatchList` / `homeworkTrimPattern` /
  `questionUsageFeed` app operations + STR strings. Frontend-only, no server/contract change. Gate: app
  `tsc --noEmit` clean + web bundle green (`expo export --platform web`). Not verified against a live server.
- Homework Tracker — app screens (frontend for HW-T1→T4): new Expo **Homework tab** (📒, gated `tracker:read`)
  with a native-stack of 4 screens consuming the existing server contract (no server/contract change).
  `HomeworkHomeScreen` — section + date aware daily dashboard: live DAY_TOTAL vs the 240 ceiling, per-subject
  declarations with >40 band warnings, and the summary roll-up (chase list with §7.2 attention/comms badges,
  open resubmissions, on-time % / chase volume / return latency, topic touches). `DeclareHomeworkScreen` —
  subject-teacher declaration form (subject chips, TOP-tags, TIME_DECL, Q_COUNT, Pool ref, revision flag;
  classLevel derived from the selected class). `HomeworkReconcileScreen` — class-teacher trim (Q_COUNT → time
  follows; rank ক/খ/গ auto-chosen) + present/absent roster + confirm-issue (over-ceiling blocks the button;
  server also enforces). `CheckingQueueScreen` — pick an item → SUBMITTED records → record RESULT (WRONG
  auto-spawns; PARTIAL on judgment) with optional Pool top-up (qids + minutes). New homework label helpers +
  STR strings; `HomeworkStackParamList` + `HomeworkTab` nav. Gate: **app `tsc --noEmit` clean + web bundle
  green** (`expo export --platform web`, 480 modules). **Not verified against a live server.**
- Homework Tracker HW-T4 (roll-ups + thresholds + question-usage feed, D-#44): new `HomeworkSummaryService`
  + GraphQL (all `tracker:read`): `homeworkSummary` (chase list + §7.2 attention/comms thresholds at
  CHASE_COUNT≥2/≥3, open resubmissions, submitted-on-time % / chase volume / Given→Returned latency,
  touches-per-TOP-tag), `homeworkWatchList` (§7.3 — students with ≥3 open/recent resubmissions in a rolling
  14-day window), `homeworkTrimPattern` (§7.4 — subjects trimmed on >30% of the month's reconciled days),
  `questionUsageFeed` (§8.4 — **de-identified** per-qid Pool usage counts across `selectedQids` + top-ups; no
  student identity, ADR-005 firewall untouched). Thresholds are the A-01/D-#34 figures; time inputs passed as
  epoch millis for deterministic math. No new vocab, no wire-contract change. Gate green: server tsc clean,
  vocab verifier PASS, **jest 223/223** (5 new in homeworkSummary.test; firewall green). Closes the homework
  build (HW-T1→T4); covers handoff §12 #10.
- Plan review/approval loop PR-3 (app screens, D-#38): new Expo **Review tab** (gated `content:review`
  OR `content:assign_review`). `ReviewHomeScreen` (role-aware inbox + my-reviews, each query paused per
  perm), `ReviewSubmitScreen` (teacher: plan render + verdict chips + feedback → `submitPlanReview`),
  `ReviewThreadScreen` (admin: round history + copy-feedback-to-clipboard + assign-next-round + approve).
  `PlanViewScreen` gains Principal assign + approve actions. New label helpers `reviewVerdictLabel`/
  `reviewRoundStatusLabel` + STR strings; nav `ReviewStackParamList` + `ReviewTab`. Frontend-only, no
  server/contract change. Gate: app `tsc --noEmit` clean + web bundle green (476 modules).
- Homework Tracker HW-T3 (resubmission + Pool top-up, D-#43): new `HomeworkResubmissionService` —
  `checkRecord` records RESULT at SUBMITTED→CHECKED; **WRONG auto-spawns** a resubmission (a NEW record,
  same `HW_ID`, `resubOf` set, fresh GIVEN→…→RETURNED pass; original → RESUBMIT), **PARTIAL** spawns only
  on the teacher's `resubmit` judgment, **CORRECT** advances with no spawn. Four §5 top-up boundaries
  enforced: (1) selected-not-authored — `TOPUP_QIDS` must resolve to existing CURRENT question artifacts in
  the same subject+class (reads the Slice-2 question store; no corpus/identity path); (2) reactive-only — a
  top-up attaches only to a spawned resubmission; (3) time-counted — `TOPUP_TIME` summed in
  `getStudentDayLoad`; (4) inside the resubmission — same `HW_ID`, `TOPUP_FLAG`. GraphQL: `checkHomeworkRecord`
  mutation (subject-teacher write-scope) + `studentDayLoad` query (per-child base+top-up minutes vs 240).
  **Fix:** dropped the HW-T1 unique index on `HomeworkStudentRecord {hwItemId, studentId}` (a resubmission is
  a legitimate 2nd record for the same student+item). No new vocab, no wire-contract change. Gate green:
  server tsc clean, vocab verifier PASS, **jest 218/218** (14 new in homeworkResubmission.test; firewall green).
- Plan review/approval loop PR-2 (D-#38): `approvePlan` — Principal sign-off `reviewed→gold`
  (`content:promote_gold`), closes the thread (supersedes any open round), audits `PLAN_APPROVED`;
  rejects unless the plan is `reviewed`. `planReviewInbox` (Principal/Office — submitted rounds, the
  `feedback` is the Claude-Desktop text) + `planReviewThread` (full round history by any artifact
  version; admins see all, a teacher only threads they reviewed). Re-import linkage: `persistEnvelope`
  supersedes the open round when a revised plan version supersedes the prior (`supersedeOpenRoundsForAddress`,
  shared by reassign/re-import/sign-off). No wire-contract change. Gate: server tsc clean, vocab verifier
  PASS, **jest 205/205** (firewall green).
- Plan review/approval loop PR-1 (D-#38/#39/#40): in-app vetting of imported plans. App-native vocab
  `content:assign_review` perm (Principal/Office), `content:review` extended to TEACHER, `REVIEW_VERDICTS`
  enum + BN labels (+ verifier checks). New `ReviewAssignment` model (address-keyed so the thread spans
  re-imported versions; identity plane, ADR-005). `ReviewService` (assign/submit/cancel + `reviewerMayReadArtifact`);
  resolvers `assignPlanReview`/`submitPlanReview`/`cancelPlanReview`/`myReviewAssignments`; reviewer
  read-scope override in the `artifact` query. APPROVE drives `draft→reviewed`; one open round per address.
  Audit `REVIEW_ASSIGNED`/`SUBMITTED`/`CANCELLED`. No wire-contract change. [0383da3]
- Homework Tracker — class-teacher designation (D-#42, resolves the D-#41 open item): added
  `Section.classTeacherId` (a TEACHER User — the daily coordinator) + `assignClassTeacher(sectionId, userId)`
  mutation (roster:manage, Principal/Office; pass null to clear, assignee must be a TEACHER). New
  `assertIsClassTeacher` guard + pure `isClassTeacher`; `trimHomeworkItem`/`confirmHomeworkDay` now gate on
  class-teacher-only (handoff §9) instead of any write-scoped teacher — Principal/Office assign rather than
  reconcile. SectionRef exposes `classTeacherId`. No new permission, no wire-contract change. Gate green:
  server tsc clean, vocab verifier PASS, **jest 195/195** (7 new in classTeacher.test; firewall green).
- Homework Tracker HW-T2 (daily 240-min reconciliation + trim log + cadence, D-#41): new app-native vocab
  `RECON_STATES` (within_ceiling/over_ceiling/reconciled), `TRIM_RANKS` (a/b/c → ক/খ/গ) + BN labels, and the
  LOCKED figures `HW_DAILY_CEILING_MIN=240`/`FLOOR=120`/`SUBJECT_BAND_MAX=40`/`DEFAULT_TIME_DECL=20` (+ verifier
  checks). New `HomeworkReconciliation` model (Layer C — one per class/day, immutable trim log: trimHw/rank/
  from/to/min). New `HomeworkReconciliationService`: `tallyDay` (live DAY_TOTAL vs 240, band warnings),
  `getTrimCandidates` (pre-ranked ক→খ→গ), `applyTrim` (cut by Q_COUNT → TIME_DECL follows proportionally,
  never extends time; rank guards; logs an immutable row; rejected once reconciled), `confirmHomeworkDay`
  (the gate: blocks if DAY_TOTAL>240, hard-blocks Fri/Sat, else issues every declared item with q>0 +
  finalises reconciled). GraphQL: homeworkDayTally/homeworkTrimCandidates queries + trimHomeworkItem/
  confirmHomeworkDay mutations (tracker:read/write). **Corrected HW-T1 over-strict TIME_DECL>40 reject →
  now allowed (band warns at tally, never blocks; only the 240 day-sum blocks), per handoff §2.1.** RBAC:
  reconcile/confirm uses tracker:write+assertCanWrite (interim); class-teacher-only narrowing deferred + routed
  to Principal (D-#41). Gate green: vocab verifier PASS, shared+server tsc clean, **jest 170/170** (16 new
  HW-T2 tests; firewall J5.6 green). Not yet committed.
- Homework Tracker HW-T1 (model + 6-stage lifecycle, D-#36/#37): app-native vocab `HW_SUBJECTS`
  (content `SUBJECTS` superset + Arabic/Islam, NOT Quran — D-#36, Quran→Quran Tracker), `LIFECYCLE_STATES` (8 atomic states for the
  §3 6 stages, D-#37), `HW_RESULTS` (সঠিক/আংশিক/ভুল) + BN labels + verifier checks. New shared lifecycle
  engine (`trackers/lifecycle.ts` — legal transition graph + guards + stage map, built once, shared with the
  future Assignment tracker) and school-day calendar (`trackers/calendar.ts` — Sun–Thu). Models: `HomeworkItem`
  (Layer A, one common sheet/class+subject+day), `HomeworkStudentRecord` (Layer B, identity-bearing lifecycle
  carrier — operational plane, ADR-005), `HomeworkSequence` (atomic year-continuous HW_ID counter).
  `HomeworkService`: HW_ID gen (HW-C{class}-{SUBJECT}-{nnnn}), declareHomeworkItem (validates §2.1 — ≥1 TOP
  tag, 0–40 TIME_DECL band, C1–5, school-night, POOL_REF), issueHomeworkItem (present→GIVEN/absent→
  ABSENT_REDELIVER), transitionRecord (one timestamped legal move, CHASE_COUNT++, →CHECKED requires RESULT,
  re-delivery shifts due date). GraphQL: declare/issue/transition mutations + homeworkItems/homeworkStudentRecords
  queries (tracker:read/write, assertCanRead/Write). Rides the existing `homework` tracker-kind — no new
  tracker-kind, no envelope/harness sync. Gate green: vocab verifier PASS, shared+server tsc clean,
  **jest 154/154** (27 new HW-T1 tests; firewall J5.6 still green). Not yet committed.
- Homework Tracker (Project-06 handoff adopted by ADR, D-#33–#35): stored the LOCKED source verbatim
  (`docs/tracker-homework-handoff.md`, PRD v1.1 incl. Amendment A-01) and authored the repo build
  contract (`docs/prd-tracker-homework.md`) — gap table vs the bare Slice-3 generic tracker, per-role
  journeys with the handoff §12 checklist as acceptance criteria, and a slice-by-slice build order
  (HW-T1 model+6-stage lifecycle → HW-T2 budget reconciliation+trim log+cadence → HW-T3 resubmission+Pool
  top-up → HW-T4 roll-ups+thresholds+question-usage feed, + cross-cut RBAC/plane/firewall). Settled the
  handoff §11 open items per A-01 (HW_ID numbering, 3-value RESULT, thresholds). Rides the existing
  `homework` tracker-kind — no new tracker-kind, no envelope-schema/harness sync. **Plan/docs only — no
  feature code yet.** Appended D-#33–#35; updated STATUS + AGENTS repo map.
- Set-assembly class guard (J3): the basket carries each question's `classLevel`, and BasketScreen now
  blocks "create set" + shows a danger Notice when the basket's question class differs from the selected
  section's class (the Question-bank class filter and the set's section are decoupled, so a Class-5 basket
  could silently land on a Class-3 section). Offers a "change section" shortcut to the Section picker.
- Question-bank import (fan-out, D-#32): a Project-04 bank JSON (a `{stimuli,questions}` collection) now imports by
  fanning out into N single-doc envelopes (one per stimulus + one per question) via new
  `server/import/build_question_envelopes.py` (the question analog of `build_envelope.py`), each run through the
  unchanged gate. subject/class_level/unit parsed from the qid/stimulus_id (mixed bank refused); `tags` copied from the
  payload; `review_status=draft` + a synthesized `address` injected; `curation_tag` supplied by the importer; the
  companion `.md`/register `.tsv` are not imported (questions are app-rendered). Supersede-not-overwrite keys on the
  item IDENTITY — **qid (questions) / stimulus_id (stimuli)**, not the shared unit address — so all 100 items coexist
  as `current` rather than collapsing onto the last one. Import is **atomic** — validate all,
  persist only if all pass, else store nothing and return the failing refs (`importQuestionBank` in `ContentService`;
  `persistEnvelope` extracted from `importEnvelope`). `importFiles` mutation gains `curationTag`/`unitTitle` args and the
  `ImportResult` gains `itemsTotal/Passed/Failed`; the app Import screen detects a bank and shows a curation picker +
  "Imported X/Y". The **register is not stored** — the live `questions()` filter + Question-bank screen are the register
  (never stale). No contract change. Executed proof: builder fanned the real C5_ENG_U09 bank into 114 envelopes,
  **114/114 PASS** through `validate_import.py`; server 127/127 (3 new bank tests), shared+server+app tsc clean,
  vocab verifier green.
- HR-1 staff records (first HR slice — prd-hr H1): added `StaffProfile` model (foundation/identity plane;
  identity+bio+employment fields; sensitive NID/bankAccount/biometricId rows). New app-native vocab
  `HR_CATEGORY`/`EMPLOYMENT_TYPE`/`EMPLOYMENT_STATUS` (+ `*_LABELS_BN`, no wire-contract twin) and a
  `staff:manage` permission granted to PRINCIPAL+OFFICE (updated the vocab verifier's OFFICE-set + HR label
  checks). Added the `staff` GraphQL query gated on `staff:manage` (default-deny to TEACHER — H1.4 row-scope)
  + `StaffRef`. Import pipeline `extract-staff.py` (both source .xlsx → gitignored staff.json) +
  `import-staff.ts` (idempotent upsert by schoolId; vocab-validates; no clears; dry-run default). Frontend:
  read-only `StaffListScreen` under the Admin tab (gated `staff:manage`) with a category filter; `STAFF_QUERY`
  + Bangla labels. Data-only (no `User` logins this slice — login optional/separate, H1.2). Identity plane only —
  no new corpus→identity path; firewall test green (124/124, shared+server+app tsc clean, vocab verifier green).
  **Loaded to Atlas + verified live:** 23 staff (21 teacher / 2 office_accounts), all with phone + biometricId.
- Roster import (real students): added a roster class-level axis (`ROSTER_CLASS_LEVELS` −1..5 = Nursery/KG/One..Five
  + `ROSTER_CLASS_LABELS_BN`) separate from the LOCKED content `class_level` (1..5, unchanged — verifier stays green);
  relaxed `Class.level` bounds to the roster range. Extended `Student` with optional operational fields

  schoolId; no clears; dry-run default, --commit to write); gitignored the source .xlsx + students.json (live PII,

- 2026-06-13 — perf: Messaging M-1 coordinator-review fixes (4 confirmed efficiency findings, no contract/behavior change). (1) `messages` resolver pre-batches receipts for the whole page via new `receiptsForMessages` ($in) so `seenBy`/`seenCount` no longer fire one (or two) queries per message — a 50-message thread open drops from ~100 round-trips to 1. (2) `myConversations` resolver pre-batches members + names via new `membersForConversations` (two $in queries total) so the per-conversation `members` field is no longer an N+1. (3) `markConversationSeen` now bounds its sweep to messages newer than the caller's latest receipt (`messageId` $gt) — a re-open re-scans/re-upserts nothing already seen instead of rescanning the full conversation every time. (4) `assertChatMember` runs its conversation + membership reads in one `Promise.all` instead of sequentially (saves a round-trip on every chat op). Field resolvers fall back to single-row fetch when a list resolver didn't pre-attach, so `conversation`/`sendMessage` paths are unchanged. Refuted in review (not changed): a claimed bulkWrite string-vs-ObjectId cast bug — Mongoose 8.24 casts bulkWrite filters. **Gate GREEN (executed in worktree):** vocab verifier PASS, shared+server tsc clean, jest 611/611 (+1 new bound test; 38 suites, firewall green), app tsc clean + expo web export green. [worktree-messaging-m1]

- 2026-06-13 — fix: Notifications N-2/N-4 coordinator-review fixes (1 confirmed security gap + 1 ops hardening). **SECURITY:** `unregisterPushDevice` was owner-unscoped — the resolver ignored the auth context and the service ran `PushDevice.updateMany({expoPushToken}, {active:false})` with no owner filter, so any authenticated user could deactivate another user's/guardian's push device by token (cross-user push silencing; register already scoped by owner — the asymmetry was the gap). Now the resolver derives the owner from `ctx.auth` (same GUARDIAN/staff split as register) and the service scopes the deactivation `{expoPushToken, ...ownerFilter}` (zero/two owners rejected). Server-side dead-token pruning is unaffected (it takes the channel's own unscoped path on Expo "DeviceNotRegistered"). **OPS:** `startNotificationTicker` now logs a loud startup WARNING if the host timezone isn't Asia/Dhaka — the tick reads server-local wall-clock, so a UTC VM would fire bell/attendance/class-note triggers ~6h off silently; the warning makes a misconfigured host obvious in logs. 3 new owner-scope tests. Two findings REFUTED in review (not changed): a claimed ObjectId-vs-string idempotency/class-teacher mismatch in AttendanceReminderService (unmarkedSections returns string sectionIds — tests prove the guard + lookup work). **Gate GREEN (executed in worktree):** vocab verifier PASS, shared+server tsc clean, jest 619/619 (+3; 39 suites, firewall green), app tsc + expo web export green. [worktree-notifications-n2-n4]

Subject own-row reads (`myConductRecords`/`myAppraisals`/`myGrievances`) via the staffMatch phone-link. **Conduct ladder (D-#113):** enforces verbal→written→final→termination (no rung-skip; gross misconduct fast-tracks), hearing recorded before finalisation, termination writes `employmentStatus → terminated` (offboarding trigger; HR-5's workflow), warnings lapse lazily (D-#21 posture) + stay on file. **Vocab (app-native, NO wire sync; HR owns vocab this cycle):** CONDUCT_STAGES/CONDUCT_RECORD_STATUSES/APPRAISAL_STATUSES/APPRAISAL_OUTCOMES/GRIEVANCE_STATUSES + BN/EN + performance:manage/performance:signoff; verifier §C.10 + OFFICE exact-list. 12 new audit kinds (OBSERVATION_SUBMITTED / APPRAISAL_* / CONDUCT_* / STAFF_TERMINATED / GRIEVANCE_* / DEVELOPMENT_LOGGED). HR firewall block extended with the five new models (green both ways). **Gate GREEN (executed):** vocab verifier PASS, shared build + shared/server tsc clean, **jest 801/801** (49 suites; 25 new in `performance.test.ts`; firewall green). **Server-only** (no app screens — the HR-1..HR-3 precedent; expo export skipped). **Not verified live.** Parked (prd-hr §6/§10): REF-11 observation rubric (curriculum-owned), per-stage warning-lapse period, offboarding workflow (HR-5). - 2026-06-13 — feat: HR-5 (offboarding, server, prd-hr §6/H6, D-#29 + build rulings D-#117/#118) — the cross-cutting exit workflow that completes the HR module (HR-1..HR-5); it COMPOSES the earlier slices, never twins them. **New `modules/hr/` model:** `OffboardingCase` (trigger + status + lastWorkingDay + embedded clearance checklist + embedded hard-held final settlement + exit interview + service-certificate flags). **Services:** `offboardingMath` (pure: `employmentStatusForTrigger`, `defaultClearanceItems`, `clearanceComplete`, `lastWorkingDayReached`), `OffboardingService` (initiate / clearance add+update / access revoke + the `runDueOffboardingRevocations` sweep / settlement compute + release / exit interview / service certificate / cancel / reads); foundation `ScopeGrantService.revokeAllGrantsForUser` (REUSED, not reimplemented). **H6.1:** the trigger sets `StaffProfile.employmentStatus` (resignation→resigned, termination→terminated [the HR-4 H5.3 entry point], fixed_term_end→contract_ended, retirement→retired). **H6.2:** a configurable clearance checklist (the three §6 categories seeded as read-time defaults, D-#97 no-seed; admin adds/updates items). **H6.3 — by the system:** access revoked on the last working day via the EXISTING N-2 ticker (no new scheduler; the no-cron/lazy posture D-#20/#21) — `runDueOffboardingRevocations` runs once-per-day from the tick, disabling the `User` login + revoking ALL scope grants (idempotent, lazy date-gate); a manual admin path exists too. **H6.4:** `computeFinalSettlement` composes salary pro-rated to last day + arrears + **full leave encashment** (HR-2 `balancesForStaff`) − **outstanding advance** (HR-3 `activeAdvanceByStaff`, full one_shot netting via the payrollMath net-pay guard), **HARD-HELD until clearance is complete** (`releaseFinalSettlement`, payroll:approve/Principal, throws unless every item done/waived — D-#29; - 2026-06-15 — feat: Classroom Observation CO-6 review scheduler (server, prd-classroom-observation §CO-6 + build ruling D-#230) — the SIXTH CO slice: the tier-driven cadence scheduler that SUGGESTS who's due for a review (never auto-assigns, §CO-6 guardrail). **Server-only; the ONLY shared change is `shared/vocab.ts`** (`SUPPORT_TIERS` [STRONG/DEVELOPING/NEEDS_SUPPORT] + BN/EN labels, app-native, NO wire twin — D-#46; new verifier **§C.16c**); NO new permission (reuses `observation:read`/`observation:manage`). **All DERIVED (D-#85):** pure `deriveTier` (REF-11 → a gate BREACH or any domain at level 1 ⇒ NEEDS_SUPPORT, all domains ≥3 ⇒ STRONG, else DEVELOPING; Quran → avg rating ≥4 + full compliance ⇒ STRONG, avg ≤2.5 or <½ compliance ⇒ NEEDS_SUPPORT, else DEVELOPING — derived from REVIEW DATA ONLY, §CO-6 guardrail) + pure `intervalForTier` (DEVELOPING = base, STRONG = base×strongMultiplier [longest], NEEDS_SUPPORT = base×needsSupportMultiplier [shortest], clamped up to the `minIntervalDays` frequency cap). `dueForReview(now)` (deterministic-in-`now`) — candidates = teachers with REAL teaching sessions (distinct `teacherId` over active, non-break RoutineSlots); each tiered off their MOST RECENT released review; only DUE/overdue (+ never-reviewed, soonest bucket) returned, ranked never-reviewed → weakest tier → most-overdue. **Config:** `ObservationScheduleConfig` singleton (read-time defaults `DEFAULT_SCHEDULE_CONFIG` = base 30 / STRONG ×2 / NEEDS_SUPPORT ×0.5 / cap 7, NO seed write — D-#97, the CO-3 escalation-config precedent); `setScheduleConfig` validates (base/cap whole-days ≥1, strongMultiplier ≥1, needsSupportMultiplier in (0,1]) + audits `OBSERVATION_SCHEDULE_CONFIG_SET`. **Resolvers (`observationSchedule.ts`):** `observationDueList` + `observationScheduleConfig` (queries) + `setObservationScheduleConfig` (mutation) — **all gated `observation:manage`** (D-#230: the §CO-6 "Principal/Office/observers" intent narrowed to manage — no permission distinguishes a senior-teacher observer from a plain TEACHER, and gating the cross-teacher list to `observation:read` would expose every teacher's cadence to ALL staff, breaking the "not wider staff" guardrail). 1 new audit kind (Audit.ts server-local union); CO firewall block auto-covers (staff-only, no corpus path). **Gate GREEN (executed):** shared build + vocab verifier PASS (incl. §C.16c), shared/server tsc clean, **jest 1305/1305** (76 suites; +1 new suite `observationScheduler.test.ts` [25 — the pure tier/interval branches, the due-list ranking + most-recent-review + not-yet-due omission, the config validation, the manage RBAC] over the 1280 base; firewall green). **Server-only** (app surface is the CO app rider). **Not verified live.** **Next = CO-7** (reviewer effectiveness). [slice/co6]

- 2026-06-15 — fix(server): **PDF export of Bengali plans no longer fails — and can no longer crash the server.** Root cause = a **fontkit 2.0.4 GPOS bug**: `getAnchor()` dereferenced a NULL mark anchor while shaping **reph** conjuncts (র্ + consonant — পদার্থ/ধর্ম/নির্বাচিত, pervasive in real content), throwing `Cannot read properties of null (reading 'xCoordinate')`; the un-try/caught PDF route then took down the whole Node process (Express 4 doesn't catch async rejections). The reph-free smoke test masked it. The full Google-Fonts Noto Sans Bengali crashes identically — a library bug, not a font-subset artifact. **Fix (D-#254):** one-line null-guard in `getAnchor` shipped as `patches/fontkit+2.0.4.patch` + adopt **patch-package** (root `postinstall: patch-package`, new devDep) so it reapplies on every install/deploy; both PDF routes (`/pdf/artifact/:id`, `/pdf/set/:id`) now wrap the render in try/catch → HTTP 500 instead of a crash; + a reph-heavy regression test. **Gate GREEN (executed):** server tsc + **jest 1450/1450** (87 suites, +1 reph test); live `GET /pdf/artifact/<chapter-plan>` → 200 application/pdf, 31,939 bytes. [feat/content-browse-pdf-fix → PR into dev]

- 2026-06-17 — fix(trackers): drop the stale UNIQUE index `hwItemId_1_studentId_1` that blocked the homework Wrong→resubmission spawn (E11000). The current schema marks it non-unique (a resubmission is a 2nd record per student+item, keyed by `resubOf`), but DBs seeded under the earlier unique schema kept the old index (Mongoose names an index by keys → never recreated it). New idempotent `server/scripts/migrate-hw-record-index.ts` drops it + `syncIndexes()` recreates it non-unique; run per database. **Ran on scdhub_local (verified before/after).** dev/prod still carry the stale index → run there before the resubmission path is used. No code/contract change. [feat/meaningful-errors-and-hw-lifecycle, from homework live testing]

- 2026-06-20 — fix(server): content import FAILed in production while passing locally. The import gate spawns the Python harness via `execFile` with a hardcoded `python`; the Ubuntu prod VM has no bare `python` (only `python3`), and Windows ships only `python` (its `python3` is a Store stub) — so the one literal worked on the dev laptop and ENOENT-failed on prod. New `PYTHON_BIN` resolves the interpreter by platform (`win32`→`python`, else `python3`) with a `PYTHON_BIN` env override, applied to all three harness call sites (validate_import / build_envelope / build_question_envelopes). - 2026-07-06 — feat(observation): CO-8 publish gate (D-#271) — reverses PRD §1.3 "no sign-off". reviewObservation → REVIEWED is now observer/Principal-only; new publishClassroomObservation (observation:manage) stamps publishedAt/publishedBy and releases to the teacher. Teacher read-scope, respond, CO-7 rating, and the CO-3 escalation clock all gate on publishedAt (additive flag, not a new state). NOTIFICATION_KINDS += OBSERVATION_READY_TO_PUBLISH. One-time backfill script publishedAt=reviewedAt for already-released rows.
- 2026-07-06 — feat(observation): CO-9 parallel multi-reviewer co-review + compare (D-#272) — requestCoReviewObservation creates a NEW independent ASSIGNED row on the same recording without superseding (unlike re-review); classroomObservationsForRecording powers a Principal side-by-side compare view (per-reviewer REF-11/Quran scores, within-one-level divergence highlight, per-row publish). Guards: co-observer ≠ observed teacher, no duplicate reviewer per recording, source must have footage.
- 2026-07-07 — feat(assignment): activate guardian-chase in-app notifications — register `ASSIGNMENT_CHASE` in `NOTIFICATION_KINDS` + BN/EN labels + verifier §C.5 (D-#273). The AS-T4 emit() seam was dormant behind the unregistered kind (every in-app ladder step logged SKIPPED); no emitter/assignment code changed at activation. Also: BUG-016 (schedule Class dropdown + backend guard now accept Nursery/KG, roster range -1..5) and BUG-017 (home "delivered" flag re-keyed on (section×subject) so it no longer disagrees with the deliver screen after an entry is re-added). Gate: shared build + vocab verifier PASS, shared/server/app tsc clean, jest 1604/1604 (97 suites).
- 2026-07-07 — feat(routine): Group members admin screen — assign students to Quran/Arabic SubjectGroups (BUG-018). Root cause of guardians not seeing Quran/Arabic on the weekly routine was ZERO SubjectGroupMembership rows (verified read-only on prod+local: 0/91) + no app UI to create them; the childRoutine merge was already correct. Added `subjectGroupMemberProfiles(groupId)` (members with names, routine:read), app ops ADD/REMOVE_GROUP_MEMBER, and the Admin → Group members screen (routine:manage, reuses existing addGroupMember/removeGroupMember). End-to-end verified with a self-cleaning script: assigning a student to a Quran+Arabic group makes P1/P2 QURAN + P3 ARABIC appear on the guardian routine (net-zero DB). Data entry (placing 91 students) remains a school task. Gate: server+app tsc clean, jest 1604/1604 (97 suites).
- 2026-07-07 — feat(routine): enforce ≤1 Quran + ≤1 Arabic group per student at the DB level. `addGroupMember` already rejected a second same-track group with a friendly error, but that check was app-level (not race/script-proof). Denormalized `track` onto SubjectGroupMembership + added a UNIQUE (studentId, track) index, so the database itself refuses a second same-track membership. Migration `migrate-subgroup-membership-track.ts` backfills track then builds the index (MUST run before the new server starts against a DB with data). Verified: a raw insert of Khadija into a 2nd Quran group is rejected with E11000 on studentId_1_track_1. Gate: server tsc clean, jest 1604/1604 (97 suites).
- 2026-07-07 — feat(assignment): AS-T6 weekly load ceiling — 3-hour (180-min) hard cap per section per week (D-#274). Mirrors the homework daily-ceiling gate, weekly + on the delivered week. deliverAssignment now DRAFTS the item (stores the roster + estMinutes, no student records); confirmAssignmentWeek(section, week) sums the week's estMinutes, HARD-BLOCKS over AS_WEEKLY_CEILING_MIN=180, else issues every DRAFT item's per-student records and flips ISSUED. New assignmentWeekLoad (reconcile read) + setAssignmentItemMinutes (trim), gated to the section class teacher OR roster:manage. Collect/Check gate on ISSUED; delivery rate counts ISSUED. App: est-minutes input on deliver, DRAFT items route to a new AssignmentReconcile screen (per-subject minutes vs 180, trim, confirm). Migration migrate-assignment-item-status.ts marks pre-AS-T6 items ISSUED (run before an AS-T6 server serves a DB with old items; done on local). Gate: shared build + vocab verifier PASS, tsc x3 clean, jest 1611/1611 (97 suites).
- 2026-07-07 — fix(assignment): week numbering follows the calendar month, not the term anchor (D-#275). Weeks are now calendar Sun–Sat weeks; Week 1 = the week containing the 1st of the month, resetting monthly (a month has Weeks 1–4/5). The 4-week rotation slot follows week-of-month (`cycleWeek = ((weekOfMonth−1) mod 4)+1`; a 5th week wraps to slot 1). weekNumber stays a continuous calendar-week index for storage; the home shows "Month · Week X". No migration. Gate: shared build + verifier PASS, tsc ×3, jest 1612/1612.
- 2026-07-08 — feat(assessment): question-set authoring UX + question read-gate fix (D-#276). (1) fix(questions): `questions`/`question`/`stimuli` now authorize TEACHER reads via `buildContentScope`/`contentScopeAllows` (subject-code + classLevel) instead of the section-keyed `assertCanRead(ctx,"",subject,subject)` that could never match a teaching/proxy grant (only PRINCIPAL/OFFICE/whole_school passed) — a scope-holding teacher can finally read the bank. (2) feat(sets): Set detail renders the full question + answer (batched `payloadJson` on the `assessmentSet` query); DRAFT sets gain add (Question Bank "add to set" mode via `addQuestionToSet`) / remove (`removeQuestionFromSet`, draft-only) while ASSEMBLED stays locked; PDF export gains `?answers=&marks=` toggles (default OFF = student paper — MCQ options always print, ✓/other carriers gate on answers, per-question [marks]+total gate on marks). (3) feat(sets): optional set NAME — `createSet(name)` + new `renameSet` (any status) + shown in Set list / detail / print picker (fallback to type/id). (4) fix(app): section-picker stays in-stack + `SectionPickerScreen` guards `goBack` with `canGoBack` + AssembleSet handoff uses `initial:false` + a web nav-state sanitizer strips transient picker routes on restore (kills the persisted "Sets→SectionPicker" bounce-to-Today); assembled sets no longer show a stale draft (`cache-and-network`). (5) Question Bank full-bleed width + inline expandable details (open by default; shared `AnswerCarrier`). No vocab/contract change. Gate: server+app tsc clean, jest 1619/1619 (97 suites, +`removeQuestionFromSet` ×4, +`renameSet` ×3).
- 2026-07-09 — feat(class-test): Class Test Tracker v2 (CT-6..CT-10) + editing/publish rulings (D-#277). Fixes: `Class×subject analysis` mojibake; result-entry errors now inline+toast; published results edit-locked; WhatsApp send gated to Office/Principal. (CT-6) read-only "view all" results screen — marks + weakness + teacher/guardian action + release state — from the Class Test home. (CT-7) cumulative per-student comments by subject on the profile. (CT-8) APPROVAL GATE: teacher `submitClassTestExam` (per-exam) → SUBMITTED (guardian not visible); Office/Principal `publishClassTestExam` = approve+deliver (requires submit), `sendBackClassTestExam(reason)` → draft, teacher `recallClassTestExam`; publish/unpublish moved `tracker:write`→`roster:manage`; edit-lock extends to submitted; unpublish retracts to draft. New result fields submittedAt/submittedBy/sendBack*; audit kinds SUBMITTED/RECALLED/SENT_BACK. (CT-9) dependency-free View-based % bar charts per subject on the profile. (CT-10) derived analytics: trajectory slope, consistency, at-risk, pass/fail streak, best/weak subject, recurring-weakness tally, latest-exam rank. PRD docs/prd-tracker-class-test-v2.md. Deferred: cross-tracker whole-picture + guardian trajectory summary. No vocab/contract change. Gate: shared build + server+app tsc clean, jest 1629/1629 (97 suites, +CT-8 submit/recall/sendBack/approve + edit-lock), expo web export green.

- 2026-07-25 | Fix | **Production regression from SP-3, caught on the live site: the Homework / Assignment / Attendance / Class Test / Admin tabs all died with "Something went wrong".** The SP-3 registration inserted `<Stack.Screen name="StudentProfile">` as the **first** screen of those five navigators, and React Navigation treats a stack's first registered screen as its **initial route** — so each tab mounted the profile instead of its home screen, with `params === undefined`, and the screen's direct `useRoute().params` destructure threw on mount. Neither typecheck nor the web export could catch it (the param type is a required string and every call site passes it; only the ROUTER supplies undefined, at runtime). Fixes: (a) StudentProfile moved to LAST in all five navigators so each stack's real home is its initial route again; (b) the screen no longer trusts params — reads them via `?? {}`, keeps every hook unconditional, pauses its queries without a `studentId`, and renders an empty state instead of throwing, so a recurrence is cosmetic rather than a dead tab; (c) a regression test (`navInitialRoute.test.ts`, in the server suite because that is what CI gates and the app workspace has no runner) asserting no param-requiring screen is any stack's first screen — proven honest by reintroducing the bug in one navigator and watching it fail by name, and carrying a parser-found-something assertion that immediately caught a first-attempt line-based parser silently reading multi-line `<Stack.Screen>` tags as empty navigators. Gate: app tsc; expo web export exit 0 (full log inspected); server jest 136 suites / 2221 green. | student-profile-nav-hotfix

- 2026-08-01 | Feature | The Principal can see free-tier headroom before a ceiling stops the school (D-#414, SH-1..SH-3, owner ask). New Admin screen (gated audit:read — Principal-only, no new permission) with three cards: Atlas storage, the VM, and Google Drive. All three were MEASURED live before designing, and the assumption was wrong both ways: Drive — which holds every uploaded byte, StoredFile being only a handle — sits at 1.33 TB of a 100 TB pooled Workspace quota and is not a constraint, while Atlas M0 is the real ceiling at 76.2 MB of 512 MB. The decisive detail: the Atlas cap is per CLUSTER, not per database, and the cluster carries scdhub_prod (41.7 MB) plus scdhub_local (18.2 MB) and scdhub_dev (16.4 MB) — the test copies eat ~45% of usage, i.e. production headroom — so the panel reads cluster-wide, lists every database, and shows the top 10 collections of the current one. Egress needed a design rather than a reading: Linux exposes counters only since boot, so a daily NetSnapshot (captured by the existing notification ticker — no new scheduler — above the school-day gate, idempotent on the date key) turns them into deltas; a reading lower than the previous day is a reboot, contributing its raw counter and flagging the month partial, and a single snapshot yields null rather than a fake zero. Bands 70% amber / 85% red; a missing limit yields unknown, never a false all-clear. Every section carries its own error and the query still answers, so one dead probe never hides the numbers that were fine; Drive is cached 5 min so refreshing cannot hammer Google. Read-only by design — no prune button. Counters only: no identity document read, and no connection string, credential, or Google account address in the payload. 26 new BN/EN strings. Server+app typecheck clean; vocab verifier PASS; jest 2511/2511 (148 suites, 16 new); expo web export exit 0; the real service run against the live cluster. CI caught what the local gate could not: /proc/net/dev EXISTS on Linux, so the daily capture reached mongoose in a suite with no DB connection and buffered 10s per tick (6 timeouts); the capture now returns early unless the connection is live — which is also right in production, where the ticker starts before the DB is up. | system-health
- 2026-08-01 | Feature | System health gains a trend, a projection, a heartbeat, a prune list and a backup (D-#416, SH-4..SH-7, owner ask). Charts were the ask; the useful part was not waiting a month for data — every large collection carries a creation time (`_id` embeds one regardless), so document counts are walked backwards **exactly** and the chart opens with ~7 weeks of real curve. Bytes cannot be reconstructed, so a backfilled day is priced at `count × today's bytes-per-document`, flagged `estimated`, drawn muted **and** captioned — colour alone never separates a measured point from a derived one. Running it live caught what review had not: the series jumped 9 MB → 76 MB on deploy day, because a reconstructed day sums the tracked collections of ONE database while a measured day reports the whole-CLUSTER total the cap counts — two quantities on one axis. Fixed with a constant baseline for the part the walk cannot see, putting every point in the unit of the limit. The projection refuses to guess: under three points is not a trend, flat/shrinking has no crossing date, past-the-limit is zero not a negative, nulls are gaps not zeroes. SH-5: the ticker heartbeat is on the panel (amber 150s, red 600s) with the consequence in words — a stall silently stops homework auto-DUE/auto-ISSUE, reminders, class-note prompts, sweeps and escalations; the heartbeat moved to a leaf module because the health service reports it while the scheduler imports the health service (a require cycle tsc compiles and Node resolves to undefined by load order). SH-6: prunable estimates from an ALLOWLIST — `audits` excluded by rule (ADR-008 append-only), school records excluded as product not exhaust, both pinned by tests; report-only, no prune button. SH-7: a weekly streamed gzip-NDJSON dump to Drive (no mongodump to provision), retention of 4 touching only this job's files and running only after a successful upload, `ageDays` from the last SUCCESS so a nightly failure cannot look fresh, failures recorded — and OFF behind `BACKUP_ENABLED=1`, because a job that writes to Drive on a schedule starts when a person decides. New TrendSparkline component (View-based, no charting dependency, matching the existing MiniCharts house style). 23 new BN/EN strings. Server+app typecheck clean; jest 2537/2537 (151 suites, 19 new); expo web export exit 0; capture+backfill verified end to end against the dev database (53 reconstructed days, continuous series, projection 2036-09-21). | system-health-trends
